The file's header must be exactly `account,name`. On success the command prints\n`table,loaded` then `accounts,N`, where N is the number of rows loaded. A header-only file loads\nzero rows and exits 0. An unreadable file exits 1. Any rejected row exits 3 and the store is left\nunchanged.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Argument form: `ledger load accounts <csv>`.\n- Header must match `account,name` in that order; otherwise the file is rejected with exit 3.\n- Each data row is validated with the code rule (1 to 12 characters from `A-Z` and `0-9`) and the\n  name rule (1 to 40 characters, no comma, no newline). A duplicate code, whether within the file\n  or already present in `accounts`, is a rejected row.\n- All rows are validated before the first write; any rejected row writes nothing (see\n  FEATURE-Load-Core.md).\n- Success output is CSV on stdout: `table,loaded` then `accounts,N`.\n- An unreadable file exits 1; diagnostics go to stderr.\n\n## Routes\n\n- Command: `ledger load accounts <csv>`\n\n## Reads\n\n- `accounts` (duplicate-code check)\n\n## Writes\n\n- `accounts` (insert validated rows)\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-001` — write: insert a validated account row - supplies: `account`, `name` - returns: `account`, `name`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC load-accounts-round-trip ===\nIntent: Loading a valid accounts file stores every row and reading the count back returns the loaded rows.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=python3; The file's header must be exactly\n`txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo`. On success the command prints `table,loaded` then\n`transactions,N`. Nothing is written\nunless every row passes.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Argument form: `ledger load transactions <csv>`.\n- Header must match `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo` in that order; otherwise exit 3.\n- Each row is validated: `txn_id` is a positive integer not already present; `date` passes the\n  calendar rule (`YYYY-MM-DD`); `account` and `category` must exist in their tables; `amount`\n  passes the amount rule; `memo` is 0 to 60 characters with no comma or newline.\n- The balance rule is checked over the whole batch before the first write: no account balance may\n  fall below zero after the load.\n- Any rejected row, or a balance violation, writes nothing and exits 3.\n- Success output is CSV on stdout: `table,loaded` then `transactions,N`.\n- An unreadable file exits 1; The file's header must be exactly\n`txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo`. On success the command prints `table,loaded` then\n`transactions,N`. An unknown account or category, or any invalid row, exits 3. Nothing is written\nunless every row passes.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Argument form: `ledger load transactions <csv>`.\n- Header must match `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo` in that order; otherwise exit 3.\n- Each row is validated: `txn_id` is a positive integer not already present; `date` passes the\n  calendar rule (`YYYY-MM-DD`); `account` and `category` must exist in their tables; `amount`\n  passes the amount rule; `memo` is 0 to 60 characters with no comma or newline.\n- The balance rule is checked over the whole batch before the first write: no account balance may\n  fall below zero after the load.\n- Any rejected row, or a balance violation, writes nothing and exits 3.\n- Success output is CSV on stdout: `table,loaded` then `transactions,N`.\n- An unreadable file exits 1; diagnostics go to stderr.\n\n## Routes\n\n- Command: `ledger load transactions <csv>`\n\n## Reads\n\n- `accounts`, `categories`, `transactions` (validation and balance check)\n\n## Writes\n\n- `transactions` (insert validated rows)\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TRANSACTION-001` — write: insert a validated transaction row with its file txn_id - supplies: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC load-transactions-round-trip ===\nIntent: Loading a valid transactions file after its accounts and categories stores every row with its file txn_id.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=python3; A rejected load must exit 3 and leave the\n`accounts`, `categories`, and `transactions` tables exactly as they were before the command ran.\nIt owns no new behavior; it verifies the all-or-nothing rule across the three load commands.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- A load that contains any rejected row exits 3 and writes no rows.\n- A rejected accounts reload (duplicate codes against existing rows) leaves every table unchanged.\n- A rejected transactions batch (unknown account) leaves every table unchanged.\n\n## Routes\n\n- None. This feature is assembly and verification only.\n\n## Reads\n\n- `accounts`, `categories`, `transactions` (before and after snapshots)\n\n## Writes\n\n- None.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by FEATURE-Load-Transactions.md and FEATURE-Load-Accounts.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC load-atomic-transactions ===\nIntent: A transactions batch containing one unknown account exits 3 and leaves every table unchanged.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"init\")\n    assert code == 0\n\n    d = tempfile.mkdtemp()\n    acc = os.path.join(d, \"accounts.csv\")\n    with open(acc, \"w\", encoding=\"ascii\") as f:\n        f.write(\"account,name\\nSAFE,Store Safe\\n\")\n    cat = os.path.join(d, \"categories.csv\")\n    with open(cat, \"w\", encoding=\"ascii\") as f:\n        f.write(\"category,description\\nSALES,Sales receipts\\n\")\n    assert cli_runner(\"load\", \"accounts\", acc)[0] == 0\n    assert cli_runner(\"load\", \"categories\", cat)[0] == 0\n\n    before_accounts = db.accounts.list()\n    before_categories = db.categories.list()\n    before_transactions = db.transactions.list()\n\n    bad = os.path.join(d, \"transactions.csv\")\n    with open(bad, \"w\", encoding=\"ascii\") as f:\n        f.write(\"txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo\\n1,2026-01-01,SAFE,SALES,10.00,ok\\n2,2026-01-01,NOSUCH,SALES,10.00,bad\\n\")\n\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"load\", \"transactions\", bad)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 3\n\n    assert db.accounts.list() == before_accounts\n    assert db.categories.list() == before_categories\n    assert db.transactions.list() == before_transactions\n=== END AC load-atomic-transactions ===\n\n=== AC load-atomic-accounts-reload ===\nIntent: Reloading accounts that already exist exits 3 and leaves the accounts table unchanged.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=python3;
